<chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows); iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":261,"totalLines":261,"truncated":false}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/spmv_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_hybrid_r4/spmv/spmv.cpp","time":{"start":1792660473528,"end":1792660473569}},"id":"prt_162e40651001lggsSrw5zHY802","sessionID":"ses_e9d1d4f50ffeYtRa0QADpGgmI0","messageID":"msg_162e3df78001gz51C9X4CSnXW6"}}

Let's write a function:\n\n```\nvoid initRandomMatrixLocal(index_t* localCols, index_t* localRowOffsets,\n                           index_t* globalRowStart /*out*/, index_t* localNnz /*out*/,\n                           index_t n, index_t dim, index_t rowStart, index_t rowEnd, int rank)\n```\n\nOriginal loop:\n```\nindex_t nnzAssigned = 0;\nprob = n/(dim*dim);\nsrand(8675309);\nbool fillRemaining=false;\nfor i in 0..dim:\n  rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n  for j in 0..dim:\n    numEntriesLeft = dim*dim - (i*dim+j);\n    needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n    if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) fillRemaining=true;\n    randVal = rand()/RAND_MAX;\n    if ((nnzAssigned<n && randVal<=prob) || fillRemaining) {\n      cols[nnzAssigned]=j;\n      nnzAssigned++;\n    }\n```\nNote rowDelimiters[i] set at beginning of row before decisions of that row.                                                \\\n        if (_err != cudaSuccess) {                                                \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error: %s (%s:%d)\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(_err), \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                          \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                         \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrixLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Reproduces the exact random sequence of the original serial\n//   initRandomMatrix, but only materializes the column indices and row\n//   offsets belonging to the local row range [rowStart, rowEnd). The global\n//   non-zero index at which the local range starts is returned so that the\n//   corresponding slice of the value array can be generated identically.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrixLocal(std::vector<index_t>& cols,\n                           std::vector<index_t>& rowOffsets,\n                           index_t& globalNnzStart,\n                           const index_t n, const index_t dim,\n                           const index_t rowStart, const index_t rowEnd) {\n    const index_t localRows = rowEnd - rowStart;\n    rowOffsets.assign(localRows + 1, 0);\n    globalNnzStart = n;\n\n    if (localRows == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cols.clear();\n\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    const double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator (identical to the reference implementation)\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        if (i == rowStart) {\n            globalNnzStart = nnzAssigned;\n            rowOffsets[0] = 0;\n        }\n        if (i >= rowStart && i < rowEnd) {\n            rowOffsets[i - rowStart] = nnzAssigned - globalNnzStart;\n        }\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            const index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            const index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            const double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                if (i >= rowStart && i < rowEnd) {\n                    cols.push_back(j);\n                }\n                ++nnzAssigned;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    rowOffsets[localRows] = nnzAssigned - globalNnzStart;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fillValuesLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Generates the local slice [globalStart, globalStart + count) of the\n//   non-zero values using the exact same random sequence as the serial\n//   implementation (vector values are consumed first, then the skipped\n//   prefix of the value sequence).\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fillValuesLocal(std::vector<double>& val, const index_t globalStart,\n                     const index_t count, const index_t dim, const double maxVal) {\n    srand(1);\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        (void)rand();\n    }\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < globalStart; ++i) {\n        (void)rand();\n    }\n    val.resize(count);\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        val[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpuRange\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication for the local rows in\n//   [rowBegin, rowEnd) using OpenMP across the available CPU threads.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpuRange(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowOffsets,\n                  const double* vec, const index_t rowBegin, const index_t rowEnd,\n                  double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowOffsets[i]; j < rowOffsets[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCsrKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CUDA implementation of the CSR sparse matrix-vector product. One thread\n//   processes one row. Floating point contraction is disabled at compile time\n//   so that the result is bit-identical to the scalar reference.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\n__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                              const index_t rowBegin, const index_t rowEnd,\n                              double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const index_t idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const index_t row = rowBegin + idx;\n    if (row < rowEnd) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        const index_t start = rowOffsets[row];\n        const index_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];\n        for (index_t j = start; j < end; ++j) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n        out[row] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResultsLocal\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of the local result slice against the reference\n//   using OpenMP. rowOffset is the global index of the first local row and is\n//   only used for diagnostics.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResultsLocal(const double* reference, const double* result,\n                        const index_t size, const index_t rowOffset) {\n    int valid = 1;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(&& : valid)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        bool ok;\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            ok = std::abs(res) <= MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;\n        } else {\n            ok = std::abs((res - ref) / ref) <= MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;\n        }\n        if (!ok) {\n            valid = 0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!valid) {\n        for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n            const double ref = reference[i];\n            const double res = result[i];\n            if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n                if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                    printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\",\n                           rowOffset + i, ref, res);\n                    break;\n                }\n            } else {\n                const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n                if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                    printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                           rowOffset + i, ref, res, relError);\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return valid != 0;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: chooseCpuRows\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Decides how many local rows should be handled by the OpenMP CPU threads\n//   while the remaining rows are handled concurrently by the GPU. Small\n//   problems are dominated by kernel launch overhead and benefit from more\n//   CPU participation, whereas large problems are memory bound and are best\n//   left almost entirely to the GPU.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nindex_t chooseCpuRows(const index_t localRows, const size_t nnz) {\n    if (localRows <= 1) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n    double fraction;\n    if (nnz < 50000) {\n        fraction = 0.75;\n    } else if (nnz < 500000) {\n        fraction = 0.25;\n    } else if (nnz < 5000000) {\n        fraction = 0.05;\n    } else {\n        fraction = 0.0;\n    }\n    index_t cpuRows = static_cast<index_t>(static_cast<double>(localRows) * fraction);\n    if (cpuRows >= localRows) {\n        cpuRows = localRows - 1;\n    }\n    return cpuRows;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Also size the OpenMP thread pool so\n    // that co-located ranks do not oversubscribe the host.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, const index_t rankIdx = static_cast<index_t>(rank);\n    const index_t rowStart = rankIdx * base + std::min<index_t>(rankIdx, rem);\n    const index_t localRows = base + ((rankIdx < rem) ? 1 : 0);\n    const index_t rowEnd = rowStart + localRows;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", We can manually unroll by prefetching loads:\n\n```\nindex_t j = start;\nfor (; j + 3 < end; j += 4) {\n   double v0=val[j], v1=val[j+1], v2=val[j+2], v3=val[j+3];\n   index_t c0=cols[j], c1=cols[j+1], c2=cols[j+2], c3=cols[j+3];\n   double x0=vec[c0], x1=vec[c1], x2=vec[c2], x3=vec[c3];\n   t += v0*x0; t += v1*x1; t += v2*x2; t += v3*x3;\n}\n...\n```\nThis preserves arithmetic order exactly (t += sequential) while allowing loads to overlap. But compiler may still interleave with dependency. Loads are independent and can be issued before. This could improve significantly. nvcc might already unroll? With variable loop count, maybe not. Could assign each row to a warp with lane 0 doing all arithmetic but other lanes doing prefetching? That doesn't increase arithmetic parallelism but increases warps: warp per row means 32x more warps, but only lane 0 computes, other lanes idle. Warps needed = rows (16k warps) -> plenty, but each warp only lane0 adding. Memory coalescing improved: for a row, 10 lanes load contiguous val/cols (coalesced within 32-lane segment), but different rows in different warps. General loop: each warp processes chunks of up to 32: lanes load j+chunk*32; then lane0 loops over valid k in order and accumulates. This preserves order. If chunk boundaries, segment sums are (a0+a1+...+a_{k-1}) computed sequentially starting from 0, then next segment computed starting from 0 and added to t. That is t = seg0; t = t + seg1... which is not same grouping as sequential because seg1 = (a_k + ... + a_{2k-1}) starts at 0, whereas sequential would have t already including prior. But note `fillRemaining` logic: once `numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign`, it sets fillRemaining true for the rest of the loop and never resets. Since p=0.1, needToAssign decreases; near the end, when remaining positions <= remaining nnz, fills all remaining. For the last ~few entries maybe. Since expected nnz fraction 0.1, needToAssign/remaining ~0.1 <1, so condition only triggers when very close to end if behind. For a local slice rank 0, we run the full loop to dim^2 but only store rows 0..8191. fillRemaining for later global rows doesn't affect early rows because it triggers near the end. Let's design a warp-per-row kernel with shared staging:\n\nEach warp processes one row:\n```\nfor (base = start; base < end; base += 32) {\n  int k = lane;\n  j = base + k;\n  double v = (j<end)? val[j] : 0;\n  double x = (j<end)? vec[cols[j]] : 0;\n  // store to shared\n  smem_v[k] = v; smem_x[k] = x;\n  __syncwarp();\n  if (lane==0) {\n     int len = min(32, end-base);\n     for (int t=0;t<len;t++) acc += smem_v[t]*smem_x[t];\n  }\n  __syncwarp();\n}\nif(lane==0) out[row]=acc;\n```\nThis gives parallel loads and exact sequential adds on lane0. But only lane0 does 32 adds per chunk; other lanes idle during adds. Warp throughput limited by lane0 adds ~32 cycles per 32 elements = 1 add/cycle, plus loads. Across many warps, adds execute on all SMs concurrently, so total add throughput = warps * 1 per cycle. For a large row, one warp adds sequentially at ~1/cycle (maybe 4 cycles due to shared loads + FMA? separate mul/add but no FMA). 3276 elements ~3276*2 cycles = 6.5k cycles ~4.6us per row/warp. But many warps (rows/warp) and SMs. Total work = nnz additions distributed across warps; each warp handles full rows. This is similar to thread-per-row in total adds, but loads are parallel/coalesced and latency hidden by staging. The lane0 serial add bottleneck per warp could be okay if enough warps. Number of warps = rows (for one warp per row), which for rows=8192 is 8192 warps > 82 SMs * 64 = 5248 capacity, enough. For n=32768 np=1 rows=32768 -> plenty.\n\nBut wait, using one warp per row means 32 threads per row, total threads = rows*32. For rank3 rows=8192 ->262k threads, more than thread-per-row 8192, so more parallelism/occupancy. Good. The arithmetic order is preserved exactly because lane0 adds in order within each chunk and chunks in order. Yes! This is better than our earlier warp kernel which did per-element shuffles (slow). Shared memory staging with lane0 sequential adds is efficient.\n\nHowever, lane0 does all adds serially, while 31 lanes idle. The total add work is nnz, and each warp can do 1 add/cycle (for lane0) vs thread-per-row each thread does 1 add per ~? cycles due to memory latency. With enough warps, warp-per-row may be faster. For memory-bound, the loads are the bottleneck and are now parallel/coalesced. Let's benchmark.\n\nWe need shared memory per warp: two arrays of 32 doubles = 512 bytes per warp. index_t longestLen = h_rowOffsets[gpuRowList[0] + 1] - h_rowOffsets[gpuRowList[0]];\n        for (size_t k = 1; k < gpuRowList.size(); ++k) {\n            const index_t r = gpuRowList[k];\n cpuRowList.push_back(gpuRowList[longestPos]);\n        gpuRowList[longestPos] = gpuRowList.back();\n        gpuRowList.pop_back();\n    }\n\n    const index_t gpuRows =
